RANGE: You check almost your whole range here.
   - Why so little betting: against the 75% bet he sometimes folds (pocket pairs under the top card (T♦T♥) and ace high (A♥8♥)), less than a bet of that size needs to pay with nothing behind it. He calls with top pair (A♥K♥) and middle pair (Q♥J♥), and about a fifth of your range is ahead of those hands. He raises a lot, with sets (K♥K♠) and flush draws (A♣8♣).
        [To the 75% bet he folds 34%, calls 43% and raises 23%. A bet of that size breaks even as a pure bluff when he folds 43%. 22% of your range has 50% or more against the hands that call.]
   - He holds more of the strong hands here: top pair (A♥K♥) and middle pair (Q♥J♥).
        [Hands with 65% equity or more: 19% of your range, 54% of his. Your range has 42% equity.]
   - When you check he bets about half the time, with sets (K♥K♠) and flush draws (A♣5♣).
        [After your check he bets 47% of the time.]
   - What would change it. Take the strongest fifth of hands out of his range and you would bet about a tenth of yours instead of almost none: it is those hands of his that hold you back. If he could not bet when checked to you would bet about a tenth: the chance that he bets is part of what makes your checks worth making. It would be much the same if he could not raise or without your own strongest fifth.
        [Solved again as it is, your range bets 1% of the time. Without his strongest fifth of hands: 13%. If he could not bet when checked to: 11%. If he could not raise: 5%. Without your own strongest fifth: 0%.]

== 3BP 4cQsKc7s X [IP]   (turn, reach 1.00/0.98)
RANGE: You bet about half your range here and check the other half.
   - Why the split: against the 75% bet he folds about half the time (pocket pairs under the top card (8♦8♥)), more than a bet of that size needs. He calls with top pair (A♣K♥) and middle pair (A♥Q♥), and about two fifths of your range is ahead of those hands. He raises now and then, with sets (Q♦Q♥) and flush draws (A♠T♠).
        [To the 75% bet he folds 56%, calls 31% and raises 13%. A bet of that size breaks even as a pure bluff when he folds 43%. 41% of your range has 50% or more against the hands that call.]
   - You hold more of the strong hands: top pair (A♥K♥) and middle pair (Q♥J♥). He has fewer.
        [Hands with 65% equity or more: 54% of your range, 19% of his. Your range has 59% equity.]
   - The bets are mostly sets (K♥K♠), flush draws (A♣5♣) and ace high (A♥3♥). The checks are mostly top pair (A♥K♠), pocket pairs under the top card (T♥T♠) and middle pair (Q♥J♥).
        [Bets: sets 16%, flush draws 15%, ace high 12%, overpairs 11%. Checks: top pair 29%, pocket pairs under the top card 26%, middle pair 21%, gutshots 5%.]
   - What would change it. Take the strongest fifth of hands out of his range and you would bet nearly all of yours instead of about half: it is those hands of his that hold you back. If he could not raise you would bet about three quarters: his raises keep part of your range quiet. Without your own strongest fifth you would bet only about a third: the rest of the betting leans on those hands being there.
        [Solved again as it is, your range bets 47% of the time. Without his strongest fifth of hands: 100%. If he could not raise: 74%. Without your own strongest fifth: 34%.]
